# IMPERIA COLD WAR — 1970 focus and legacy mechanic corrections.
# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Universal SSR focus programme.
COLDWAR_SSR_offer_clearing = { if = { limit = { COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_clearing = yes } set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_ever_offered country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.700 } } }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_cancel_selection_clearing = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_ever_offered }

COLDWAR_SSR_preview_offer_clearing = { # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1 MD standard: MD treasury and additional-expense lines
    effect_tooltip = {
        custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_clearing_origin set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.002 } } modify_treasury_effect = yes set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_expense_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.00003 } } custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_expense_change_tt
        newline = yes
        custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_prospective_partner_tt
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_clearing_recipient
    } }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_dispatch_clearing = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = UKR COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_clearing = yes }

        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity
        save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_UKR
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the partner records the offer in its own event
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_selected_partner = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6001 } }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_clearing_UKR = {
    UKR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.002 } } }
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_clearing_UKR_to = yes
            UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            UKR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_active
        set_country_flag = ICW_SSR_incoming_clearing
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event
        UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6101 } }
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_clearing_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_pending
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer clears its own offer in its reply event
        UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6201 } }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_terminate_clearing_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_active }
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer ends its own side in its notice event
        UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6301 } }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_pending
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UKR_active
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_terminate_origin_clearing_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_active }
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the partner ends its own side in its notice event
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6401 } }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_active
        remove_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_clearing_origin
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_clearing_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_clearing_UZB = {
    UZB = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.002 } } }
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UZB_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_clearing_UZB
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_clearing_UZB_to = yes
            UZB = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_clearing_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            UZB = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UZB_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_clearing_from_UZB_active
        set_country_flag = ICW_SSR_incoming_clearing
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event
        UZB = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6101 } }
    }
}

    effect_tooltip = {
        custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_technical_origin set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.004 } } modify_treasury_effect = yes set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_expense_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.00005 } } custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_expense_change_tt
        newline = yes
        custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_prospective_partner_tt
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_technical_recipient
    } }

        limit = { tag = TRK COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_technical = yes }
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_ever_offered
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity
        save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_technical_TRK
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the partner records the offer in its own event
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_selected_partner = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6002 } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { tag = KYR COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_technical = yes }
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_ever_offered
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity
        save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_technical_KYR
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the partner records the offer in its own event
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_selected_partner = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6002 } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { tag = TAJ COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_technical = yes }
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_ever_offered
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity
        save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_technical_TAJ

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_technical_UKR = {
    UKR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.004 } } }
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0015 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_UKR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_technical_UKR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_technical_UKR_to = yes
            UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_pending }
            num_of_factories > 4
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            UKR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_UKR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_UKR_active
        set_country_flag = ICW_SSR_incoming_technical
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event
        UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6102 } }
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_technical_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_technical_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_UKR_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_UKR_pending
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer clears its own offer in its reply event

    }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_technical_KYR = {
    KYR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.004 } } }
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0015 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_KYR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_technical_KYR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_technical_KYR_to = yes
            KYR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_technical_pending }
            num_of_factories > 4
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            KYR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_KYR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_technical_from_KYR_active
        set_country_flag = ICW_SSR_incoming_technical
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event
        KYR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6102 } }
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_preview_offer_training = { # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1 MD standard: MD treasury and additional-expense lines
    effect_tooltip = {
        custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_training_origin set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.003 } } modify_treasury_effect = yes set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_expense_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.00005 } } custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_expense_change_tt
        newline = yes
        custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_prospective_partner_tt
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_training_recipient
    } }

        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_training_UKR = {
    UKR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.003 } } }
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0015 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_UKR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_training_UKR_to = yes
            UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_pending }
            num_divisions > 5
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            UKR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_active
        set_country_flag = ICW_SSR_incoming_training
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event
        UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6104 } }
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_training_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_pending
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer clears its own offer in its reply event
        UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6204 } }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_training_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_terminate_training_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_active }
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer ends its own side in its notice event
        UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6304 } }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_pending
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_training_from_UKR_active
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_training_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_terminate_origin_training_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_active }
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the partner ends its own side in its notice event
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6404 } }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_training_active
        remove_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_training_origin
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_training_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_training_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
    }
}

        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the partner records the offer in its own event
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_selected_partner = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6005 } }
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_energy_UKR = {
    UKR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.003 } } }
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0025 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_UKR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_energy_UKR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_energy_UKR_to = yes
            UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            UKR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_UKR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_UKR_active
        set_country_flag = ICW_SSR_incoming_energy
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event
        UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6105 } }
    }
}

        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_UKR_pending
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer clears its own offer in its reply event

        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event
        KYR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6105 } }
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_energy_KYR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_energy_KYR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_KYR_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_KYR_pending
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer clears its own offer in its reply event

        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_energy_KYR

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_energy_TAJ = {
    TAJ = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.003 } } }
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0025 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_TAJ_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_energy_TAJ
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_energy_TAJ_to = yes
            TAJ = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_energy_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            TAJ = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_TAJ_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_energy_from_TAJ_active
        set_country_flag = ICW_SSR_incoming_energy
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event
        TAJ = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6105 } }
    }
}

    effect_tooltip = {
        custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_water_origin set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.002 } } modify_treasury_effect = yes set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_expense_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.00003 } } custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_expense_change_tt
        newline = yes
        custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_prospective_partner_tt
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_water_recipient
    } }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_water_KAZ = {
    KAZ = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.002 } } }
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.002 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_KAZ_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_water_KAZ
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_water_KAZ_to = yes
            KAZ = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_water_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            KAZ = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_KAZ_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_water_from_KAZ_active
        set_country_flag = ICW_SSR_incoming_water
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event
        KAZ = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6106 } }
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_offer_romanian = { if = { limit = { COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_romanian = yes } set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_ever_offered country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.706 } } }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_cancel_selection_romanian = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_ever_offered }

COLDWAR_SSR_preview_offer_romanian = { # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1 MD standard: MD treasury and additional-expense lines
    effect_tooltip = {
        custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_romanian_origin set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } } modify_treasury_effect = yes set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_expense_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.00002 } } custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_weekly_expense_change_tt
        newline = yes
        custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_SSR_prospective_partner_tt
        add_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_romanian_recipient
    } }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_dispatch_romanian = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = UKR COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_romanian = yes }
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_ever_offered
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity
        save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_romanian_UKR
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the partner records the offer in its own event
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_selected_partner = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6007 } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { tag = BLR COLDWAR_SSR_can_offer_romanian = yes }
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_ever_offered
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_activity
        save_global_event_target_as = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_romanian_BLR
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the partner records the offer in its own event
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_selected_partner = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6007 } }
    }
    if = {

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_romanian_UKR = {
    UKR = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } } }
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_romanian_UKR_to = yes
            UKR = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            UKR = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_active
        set_country_flag = ICW_SSR_incoming_romanian
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event
        UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6107 } }
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_refuse_romanian_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_pending
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer clears its own offer in its reply event
        UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6207 } }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_romanian_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_terminate_romanian_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_active }
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer ends its own side in its notice event
        UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6307 } }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_pending
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UKR_active
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_romanian_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_terminate_origin_romanian_UKR = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_active }
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the partner ends its own side in its notice event
        event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6407 } }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_active
        remove_ideas = COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_romanian_origin
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UKR
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_romanian_UKR
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
    }

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_romanian_LIT = {
    LIT = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } } }
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_LIT_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_LIT
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_romanian_LIT_to = yes
            LIT = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            LIT = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_LIT_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_LIT_active
        set_country_flag = ICW_SSR_incoming_romanian
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event

    }

        limit = { tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UZB has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UZB_pending }
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_UZB_pending
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer clears its own offer in its reply event
        UZB = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6207 } }
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_UZB
        clear_global_event_target = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_from_romanian_UZB
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_update_activity = yes
    }
}

COLDWAR_SSR_dip_accept_romanian_TRK = {
    TRK = { set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } } }
    set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.001 } }
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_TRK_pending
            is_subject = no
            tag = event_target:COLDWAR_SSR_dip_to_romanian_TRK
            COLDWAR_SSR_dip_pair_romanian_TRK_to = yes
            TRK = { COLDWAR_SSR_is_independent = yes has_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_romanian_pending }
            
            check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup compare = greater_than_or_equals }
            TRK = { check_variable = { var = treasury value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup_origin compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_setup multiply = -1 } }
        modify_treasury_effect = yes
        clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_TRK_pending
        set_country_flag = COLDWAR_SSR_dip_in_romanian_from_TRK_active
        set_country_flag = ICW_SSR_incoming_romanian
        COLDWAR_SSR_diplomacy_reconcile = yes
        # IMPERIA COLD WAR — B1: the proposer pays and adds its spirit in its own reply event
        TRK = { country_event = { id = COLDWAR_SSR_DIP.6107 } }
    }
}
